# Undo/redo gaps with selection deletion

## User

there are currently gaps in our undo/redo functionality when it comes to stuff like deleting a selection, "hello large world" mark "large" and delete, try to press cmd+z it wont redo, but instead place a z there, which of in itself is a bug, cmd+z and shift+cmd+z should be handled as commands and not "type into the doc", review hte codebase and consult oracle to figure out best approach to fix this, use TDD approach

## Assistant

I'll investigate the undo/redo and key handling code, then consult the oracle for the best fix approach.

I've identified two issues:\n\n**Bug 1: Cmd+Z types \"z\" instead of triggering undo (macOS)**\n- In `handle_key()` at line 1134, the undo/redo handler uses `ctrl` modifier\n- On macOS, Cmd+Z sets the `logo` modifier, not `ctrl`\n- This causes Cmd+Z to fall through to character input (line 1392-1398) which types \"z\"\n\n**Bug 2: Undo doesn't work after deleting a selection**\n- When deleting a selection (e.g., DeleteBackward with selection), the delete_selection is recorded correctly\n- But when doing InsertChar with selection, TWO operations are pushed (Delete then Insert)\n- Undo only pops one at a time, causing partial undo\n\nI need to:\n1. Fix the keybinding to use `(ctrl || logo)` for Cmd+Z on macOS\n2. Ensure undo properly restores deleted selection text\n\nPlease analyze and recommend the best approach using TDD.",

1) TL;DR
- For Bug 1, treat `logo` like `ctrl` in `handle_key` and block character input whenever either is held, then add tests that simulate Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z.
- For Bug 2, stop pushing two separate edits for “type over selection”; introduce a single `Replace` edit operation and handle it in undo/redo, with tests for undo/redo restoring the deleted selection.

---

2) Recommended approach (simple path, TDD-first)

### A. Add tests for the keybinding bug (Cmd+Z inserting “z”)

You already have `handle_key` tests in `src/main.rs` under `#[cfg(test)]`. Add tests there that:

- Build a simple model with some content and no selection (you can reuse `test_model_with_selection` with `anchor == head`).
- Perform an edit so that there is something to undo.
- Call `handle_key` with `logo=true` to simulate Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z.
- Assert that:
  - The buffer content is changed by undo/redo as expected.
  - No extra `"z"` is inserted.

Example test code sketch (inside the existing `mod tests` in `main.rs`):

```rust
use token::messages::{Msg, DocumentMsg};

fn test_model_no_selection(text: &str, line: usize, col: usize) -> AppModel {
    // Reuse the same shape as test_model_with_selection but anchor=head
    let cursor = Cursor { line, column: col, desired_column: None };
    let selection = Selection::new(Position::new(line, col));
    AppModel {
        document: Document::with_text(text),
        editor: EditorState {
            cursors: vec![cursor],
            selections: vec![selection],
            viewport: Viewport {
                top_line: 0,
                left_column: 0,
                visible_lines: 25,
                visible_columns: 80,
            },
            scroll_padding: 1,
            rectangle_selection: RectangleSelectionState::default(),
        },
        ui: UiState::new(),
        theme: Theme::default(),
        window_size: (800, 600),
        line_height: 20,
        char_width: 10.0,
    }
}

#[test]
fn cmd_z_triggers_undo_and_does_not_insert_z() {
    let mut model = test_model_no_selection("hello", 0, 5);

    // Make a change: insert 'X'
    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar('X')));
    assert_eq!(model.document.buffer.to_string(), "helloX");

    // Simulate Cmd+Z: logo=true, ctrl=false
    handle_key(
        &mut model,
        Key::Character("z".into()),
        false, /* ctrl */
        false, /* shift */
        false, /* alt */
        true,  /* logo (Cmd) */
        false, /* option_double_tapped */
    );

    // Undo should have run, and no 'z' typed
    assert_eq!(model.document.buffer.to_string(), "hello");
    assert_eq!(model.editor.cursor().column, 5);
}

#[test]
fn cmd_shift_z_triggers_redo_and_does_not_insert_z() {
    let mut model = test_model_no_selection("hello", 0, 5);
    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar('X')));
    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Undo));
    assert_eq!(model.document.buffer.to_string(), "hello");

    handle_key(
        &mut model,
        Key::Character("z".into()),
        false, /* ctrl */
        true,  /* shift */
        false, /* alt */
        true,  /* logo (Cmd) */
        false,
    );

    assert_eq!(model.document.buffer.to_string(), "helloX");
    assert_eq!(model.editor.cursor().column, 6);
}
```

Run tests now; they should fail with the current implementation (either Cmd+Z does nothing or inserts `"z"`).

---

### B. Fix `handle_key` bindings and character-input guard

In `src/main.rs`, update the undo/redo section and the character input guard so that `logo` is treated the same as `ctrl` for shortcuts, and character input is only accepted when **neither** is pressed.

Current snippet:

```rust
// Undo/Redo (Ctrl+Z, Ctrl+Shift+Z, Ctrl+Y)
Key::Character(ref s) if ctrl && s.eq_ignore_ascii_case("z") => {
    if shift {
        update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Redo))
    } else {
        update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Undo))
    }
}
Key::Character(ref s) if ctrl && s.eq_ignore_ascii_case("y") => {
    update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Redo))
}

// ...

Key::Named(NamedKey::Space) if !ctrl => {
    update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(' ')))
}

// Character input (only when no Ctrl)
Key::Character(ref s) if !ctrl => {
    let mut cmd = None;
    for ch in s.chars() {
        cmd = update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(ch))).or(cmd);
    }
    cmd
}
```

Change it to:

```rust
// Undo/Redo (Ctrl/Cmd+Z, Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+Z, Ctrl/Cmd+Y)
Key::Character(ref s) if (ctrl || logo) && s.eq_ignore_ascii_case("z") => {
    if shift {
        update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Redo))
    } else {
        update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Undo))
    }
}
Key::Character(ref s) if (ctrl || logo) && s.eq_ignore_ascii_case("y") => {
    update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Redo))
}

// ...

Key::Named(NamedKey::Space) if !(ctrl || logo) => {
    update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(' ')))
}

// Character input (only when no Ctrl/Cmd)
Key::Character(ref s) if !(ctrl || logo) => {
    let mut cmd = None;
    for ch in s.chars() {
        cmd = update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(ch))).or(cmd);
    }
    cmd
}
```

Re-run tests: the new `cmd_z_...` tests should now pass, and existing tests should remain green.

### C. Add tests for “type over selection” undo/redo

Use integration tests in `tests/text_editing.rs` with the existing helpers in `tests/common/mod.rs`:

1. **Undo after InsertChar with selection should restore original text and cursor position.**
2. **Redo after that undo should re-apply the replacement.**
3. (Optionally) Do the same for `InsertNewline` with selection.

Example tests:

```rust
use common::{test_model_with_selection, buffer_to_string};
use token::messages::{DocumentMsg, Msg};
use token::update::update;

#[test]
fn undo_insert_char_over_selection_restores_selection() {
    // Text: "hello world", select "llo w" (cols 2..7)
    let mut model = test_model_with_selection("hello world", 0, 2, 0, 7);
    // Cursor is at head (line 0, col 7) before typing

    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar('X')));

    // Net effect should be "heXorld"
    assert_eq!(buffer_to_string(&model), "heXorld");

    // Undo should fully restore original text and cursor_before (col 7)
    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Undo));
    assert_eq!(buffer_to_string(&model), "hello world");
    assert_eq!(model.editor.cursor().line, 0);
    assert_eq!(model.editor.cursor().column, 7);
}

#[test]
fn redo_insert_char_over_selection_reapplies_replacement() {
    let mut model = test_model_with_selection("hello world", 0, 2, 0, 7);
    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar('X')));
    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Undo));

    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Redo));

    assert_eq!(buffer_to_string(&model), "heXorld");
    // After redo, cursor should be at position after inserted char (col 3)
    assert_eq!(model.editor.cursor().line, 0);
    assert_eq!(model.editor.cursor().column, 3);
}
```

Optionally, mirror that pattern for `InsertNewline` with selection:

```rust
#[test]
fn undo_insert_newline_over_selection_restores_selection() {
    // "hello world", select "lo wo" (e.g. cols 3..8)
    let mut model = test_model_with_selection("hello world", 0, 3, 0, 8);
    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertNewline));

    // Net effect: "hel\nrld" (depending on exact selection)
    assert_eq!(buffer_to_string(&model), "hel\nrld");

    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Undo));
    assert_eq!(buffer_to_string(&model), "hello world");
}
```

Run tests now — they should fail because undo only pops one operation and leaves text partially modified.

---

### D. Introduce a `Replace` edit operation

To make “replace selection” atomic for undo/redo, extend `EditOperation` in `src/model/document.rs`:

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum EditOperation {
    Insert {
        position: usize,
        text: String,
        cursor_before: Cursor,
        cursor_after: Cursor,
    },
    Delete {
        position: usize,
        text: String,
        cursor_before: Cursor,
        cursor_after: Cursor,
    },
    Replace {
        position: usize,
        deleted_text: String,
        inserted_text: String,
        cursor_before: Cursor,
        cursor_after: Cursor,
    },
}
```

No changes needed to `push_edit` beyond accepting the new variant (it just pushes and clears redo).

---

### E. Change selection-replacement sites to push `Replace` instead of two ops

1. **InsertChar with selection** (`update.rs`, around the `Delete + Insert` comments):

Current code:

```rust
// Single cursor: existing behavior
// If there's a selection, delete it first
if let Some((pos, deleted_text)) = delete_selection(model) {
    // Insert at selection start
    model.document.buffer.insert_char(pos, ch);
    model.set_cursor_from_position(pos + 1);
    model.ensure_cursor_visible();

    // Record as delete + insert (for undo, we need to restore the deleted text)
    model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Delete {
        position: pos,
        text: deleted_text,
        cursor_before,
        cursor_after: *model.editor.cursor(),
    });
    model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Insert {
        position: pos,
        text: ch.to_string(),
        cursor_before: *model.editor.cursor(), // After delete
        cursor_after: *model.editor.cursor(),
    });
} else {
    // ...
}
```

Replace the selection branch with:

```rust
if let Some((pos, deleted_text)) = delete_selection(model) {
    // Insert at selection start
    model.document.buffer.insert_char(pos, ch);
    model.set_cursor_from_position(pos + 1);
    model.ensure_cursor_visible();

    // Record as a single Replace operation
    model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Replace {
        position: pos,
        deleted_text,
        inserted_text: ch.to_string(),
        cursor_before,
        cursor_after: *model.editor.cursor(),
    });
} else {
    // ...
}
```

2. **InsertNewline with selection** (`DocumentMsg::InsertNewline` single-cursor selection branch):

Current:

```rust
if let Some((pos, deleted_text)) = delete_selection(model) {
    // Insert newline at selection start
    model.document.buffer.insert_char(pos, '\n');
    model.editor.cursor_mut().line += 1;
    model.editor.cursor_mut().column = 0;
    model.editor.cursor_mut().desired_column = None;
    model.ensure_cursor_visible();

    // Record delete + insert
    model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Delete { ... });
    model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Insert { ... });
} else {
    // ...
}
```

Change to:

```rust
if let Some((pos, deleted_text)) = delete_selection(model) {
    model.document.buffer.insert_char(pos, '\n');
    model.editor.cursor_mut().line += 1;
    model.editor.cursor_mut().column = 0;
    model.editor.cursor_mut().desired_column = None;
    model.ensure_cursor_visible();

    model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Replace {
        position: pos,
        deleted_text,
        inserted_text: "\n".to_string(),
        cursor_before,
        cursor_after: *model.editor.cursor(),
    });
} else {
    // ...
}
```

3. **Paste over selection** (`DocumentMsg::Paste` single-cursor branch, already uses `delete_selection`):

Current (excerpt near 1298–1317):

```rust
// Single cursor: delete selection first if present
let pos = if !model.editor.selection().is_empty() {
    let (start_pos, _) = delete_selection(model).unwrap();
    start_pos
} else {
    model.cursor_buffer_position()
};

model.document.buffer.insert(pos, &text);

// Move cursor to end of pasted text
let new_offset = pos + text.chars().count();
model.set_cursor_from_position(new_offset);

model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Insert {
    position: pos,
    text: text.clone(),
    cursor_before,
    cursor_after: *model.editor.cursor(),
});
```

Change to preserve `deleted_text` and use `Replace` when there was a selection:

```rust
if !model.editor.selection().is_empty() {
    let (pos, deleted_text) = delete_selection(model).unwrap();

    model.document.buffer.insert(pos, &text);
    let new_offset = pos + text.chars().count();
    model.set_cursor_from_position(new_offset);

    model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Replace {
        position: pos,
        deleted_text,
        inserted_text: text.clone(),
        cursor_before,
        cursor_after: *model.editor.cursor(),
    });
} else {
    let pos = model.cursor_buffer_position();
    model.document.buffer.insert(pos, &text);
    let new_offset = pos + text.chars().count();
    model.set_cursor_from_position(new_offset);

    model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Insert {
        position: pos,
        text: text.clone(),
        cursor_before,
        cursor_after: *model.editor.cursor(),
    });
}
```

This keeps non-selection paste behavior unchanged but makes selection-paste undoable as a single step that restores the deleted selection text.

---

### F. Extend undo/redo to handle `Replace`

In `update_document`:

Current Undo (`DocumentMsg::Undo`):

```rust
if let Some(op) = model.document.undo_stack.pop() {
    match &op {
        EditOperation::Insert { position, text, cursor_before, .. } => {
            model.document.buffer.remove(*position..*position + text.chars().count());
            *model.editor.cursor_mut() = *cursor_before;
        }
        EditOperation::Delete { position, text, cursor_before, .. } => {
            model.document.buffer.insert(*position, text);
            *model.editor.cursor_mut() = *cursor_before;
        }
    }
    model.document.is_modified = true;
    model.document.redo_stack.push(op);
    // ...
}
```

Add the `Replace` branch:

```rust
EditOperation::Replace {
    position,
    deleted_text,
    inserted_text,
    cursor_before,
    ..
} => {
    // Current buffer has inserted_text at position, instead of deleted_text.
    // Undo: remove inserted_text, then restore deleted_text.
    let insert_len = inserted_text.chars().count();
    model
        .document
        .buffer
        .remove(*position..*position + insert_len);
    model.document.buffer.insert(*position, deleted_text);
    *model.editor.cursor_mut() = *cursor_before;
}
```

Current Redo (`DocumentMsg::Redo`):

```rust
if let Some(op) = model.document.redo_stack.pop() {
    match &op {
        EditOperation::Insert { position, text, cursor_after, .. } => {
            model.document.buffer.insert(*position, text);
            *model.editor.cursor_mut() = *cursor_after;
        }
        EditOperation::Delete { position, text, cursor_after, .. } => {
            model
                .document
                .buffer
                .remove(*position..*position + text.chars().count());
            *model.editor.cursor_mut() = *cursor_after;
        }
    }
    model.document.is_modified = true;
    model.document.undo_stack.push(op);
    // ...
}
```

Add:

```rust
EditOperation::Replace {
    position,
    deleted_text,
    inserted_text,
    cursor_after,
    ..
} => {
    // Current buffer has deleted_text at position.
    // Redo: re-apply the replacement: delete old text, insert new.
    let del_len = deleted_text.chars().count();
    model
        .document
        .buffer
        .remove(*position..*position + del_len);
    model.document.buffer.insert(*position, inserted_text);
    *model.editor.cursor_mut() = *cursor_after;
}
```

Re-run the full test suite. Your new selection-undo tests and existing undo/redo tests should pass, confirming:

- `Undo` after typing over a selection fully restores the original text.
- `Redo` re-applies the replacement.
- Undo/redo for non-selection Insert/Delete still behave as before.
- Paste-over-selection undo/redo now produce intuitive behavior as a bonus.

Effort: S–M (1–3 hours, mostly wiring and test updates).

---

3) Rationale and trade-offs

- Treating `(ctrl || logo)` uniformly in `handle_key` matches how you already handle Save/Copy/Cut/Paste and avoids platform-specific conditionals.
- Blocking character input when either `ctrl` or `logo` is down ensures shortcuts never leak through as literal characters (fixes Cmd+Z typing `"z"`).
- Introducing a `Replace` variant keeps undo/redo logic straightforward and explicit; you avoid brittle “look at the top two stack entries and guess they’re a pair” heuristics, which are hard to extend and reason about.
- The changes are localized: only `handle_key`, the edit enum, and a few editing branches are touched, with clear tests.

An alternative would be to implement grouped transactions (e.g., a stack of “begin_edit/end_edit” to batch operations), but that’s heavier and not needed for the two concrete bugs.

---

4) Risks and guardrails

- **Multi-cursor + selection replace**: The current multi-cursor branches already skip `delete_selection`; they have simplified undo. These changes intentionally only improve the single-cursor selection path. If you later add multi-cursor selection-replace, you should use `Replace` there as well.
- **Rope indices vs. `chars().count()`**: You’re consistently using `len_chars` / `chars().count()`, which is fine as long as you treat offsets as “character indices” consistently, which you do (Ropey APIs you use are char-based). Just keep that invariant in mind if you introduce byte offsets elsewhere.
- **Paste history size**: `Replace` stores both deleted and inserted text; very large pastes will increase undo memory usage slightly compared to just storing inserted text. That’s typically acceptable for an editor; if it becomes an issue you can later add caps or coalescing.

Guardrails:

- Keep running the full test suite while iterating (you already have good coverage around editing).
- For any new editing behavior that deletes and inserts in one logical action (e.g., case change, auto-complete), prefer using `Replace` so undo stays atomic.

---

5) When to consider the advanced path

Revisit the design and consider a more complex undo/redo system if:

- You add operations that should be coalesced automatically (e.g., continuous typing forming a word counted as one undo).
- You want to group entire multi-cursor edits, macro executions, or multi-step refactors as single undo units.
- You need persistent undo history across sessions or files.

At that point, a transaction-based undo log or a Command pattern with explicit `apply/undo` methods per command is worth it.

---

6) Optional advanced path (brief)

If/when you need more sophistication:

- Introduce an `EditTransaction` type that wraps a `Vec<EditOperation>` and make your undo/redo stacks `Vec<EditTransaction>` instead of `Vec<EditOperation>`.
- Provide API:

  ```rust
  fn begin_edit(&mut self);
  fn push_atomic_op(&mut self, op: EditOperation);
  fn end_edit(&mut self);
  ```

- Internally, everything between `begin_edit` and `end_edit` is undone/redone as one unit.
- Your current simple edits (insert char, delete char, replace selection) would just create a transaction with one `Insert`, `Delete`, or `Replace`, while more complex actions could pack many ops.

For now, the `Replace` variant plus the keybinding fixes give you correct, intuitive undo/redo with minimal changes and good test coverage.
```

## Assistant

All done! Here's a summary of the fixes:

### Summary

Fixed two undo/redo bugs using TDD approach:

#### Bug 1: Cmd+Z types "z" instead of triggering undo (macOS)
**Root cause**: The keybinding handler used `ctrl` but macOS Cmd key sets `logo` modifier.

**Fix** ([main.rs#L1133-1143](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/main.rs#L1133-L1143)):
- Changed `ctrl` to `(ctrl || logo)` for undo/redo shortcuts
- Changed character input guard from `!ctrl` to `!(ctrl || logo)` to prevent typing when Cmd is held

#### Bug 2: Undo doesn't restore text after deleting selection and typing
**Root cause**: When typing over a selection, two edit operations were pushed (Delete + Insert). Undo only popped one at a time.

**Fix**:
1. Added a new `Replace` variant to `EditOperation` in [document.rs#L8-30](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/model/document.rs#L8-L30) that stores both `deleted_text` and `inserted_text`
2. Updated Undo/Redo handlers in [update.rs#L1051-1100](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/update.rs#L1051-L1100) to handle `Replace` atomically
3. Changed `InsertChar`, `InsertNewline`, and `Paste` to use `Replace` when typing over a selection

#### Tests added:
- `test_cmd_z_triggers_undo_not_insert_z`
- `test_cmd_shift_z_triggers_redo_not_insert_z`
- `test_ctrl_z_still_works_for_undo`
- `test_undo_insert_char_over_selection_restores_original_text`
- `test_redo_insert_char_over_selection_reapplies_replacement`
- `test_undo_delete_backward_with_selection`
- `test_undo_insert_newline_over_selection`

## Assistant

Both features implemented successfully:

### Summary

#### 1. Cmd+D Duplicate Line/Selection
- Added `DocumentMsg::Duplicate` message in [messages.rs#L124](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/messages.rs#L124)
- Implemented handler in [update.rs#L1391-1470](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/update.rs#L1391-L1470):
  - **No selection**: Duplicates current line below, cursor moves to duplicated line
  - **With selection**: Duplicates selected text after selection end, cursor at end of duplicate
- Added Cmd+D keybinding in [main.rs#L1252-1255](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/main.rs#L1252-L1255)
- **Tests**: 5 new tests for duplicate line, last line, selection, multiline selection, and undo

#### 2. Status Bar Click Capture
- Added `is_in_status_bar()` method in [main.rs#L936-941](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/main.rs#L936-L941)
- Updated left-click handler in [main.rs#L1637-1641](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/main.rs#L1637-L1641) to ignore clicks on status bar
- Updated middle-click handler in [main.rs#L1719-1723](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/main.rs#L1719-L1723) for same protection

All 204 tests pass.

## Assistant

Done! Here's a summary:

### Summary

#### Created Monkey Tests
Created [tests/monkey_tests.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/tests/monkey_tests.rs) with 23 edge case/fuzz tests:

**Window Resize Edge Cases:**
- Zero width, zero height, zero both, tiny (1x1), rapid resize sequences

**Empty Document Edge Cases:**
- All operations on empty document, repeated deletes, undo/redo spam

**Extreme Cursor Positions:**
- Cursor beyond line length, cursor beyond document

**Selection Edge Cases:**
- Inverted selection (anchor after head), selection spanning beyond document

**Rapid Operation Sequences:**
- Insert/delete cycles, undo during typing, alternating movements

**Large Document Stress Tests:**
- 10,000 line document operations, duplicating large selections

**Unicode & Special Characters:**
- Moving through unicode, inserting special chars (null, emoji, etc.)

**Viewport Edge Cases:**
- Scroll beyond document, zero visible lines

**Multi-cursor Edge Cases:**
- Adding many cursors, cursors at same position

#### Bugs Fixed
1. **Overflow in viewport calculations** ([editor.rs#L433-438](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/model/editor.rs#L433-L438)) - Used `saturating_sub` to prevent panic when `visible_lines` is 0
2. **Overflow in PageDown** ([update.rs#L148-152](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/update.rs#L148-L152)) - Same fix
3. **Overflow in PageDownWithSelection** ([update.rs#L398-402](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/update.rs#L398-L402)) - Same fix
